TL;DR: 提出了OOD-RL-Bench框架，用于评估强化学习轨迹中的异常检测方法，在LunarLander-v3环境中验证了不同异常类型下检测器的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准主要针对图像分类器或静态低维数据集，无法捕捉RL轨迹中复杂的、依赖于动作的时间结构，缺乏评估RL场景下OOD检测器的标准化框架。

Method: 设计OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器与异常注入器；在LunarLander-v3环境中使用DQN策略，注入多种异常类型（观测扰动、状态切换、观测延迟、动作条件动力学变化等），采用匹配时间AUROC、AUPRC、假阳性率、检测延迟和分段onset指标评估检测器性能。

Result: 观测扰动和状态切换能被多种方法高精度识别；观测延迟和动作条件动力学变化即使比较同时间步的异常后分数与清洁分数，仍然难以检测。不同异常类型下检测器性能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench为评估RL轨迹中OOD检测器提供了可扩展的标准化框架，揭示了当前检测方法在时序依赖异常上的局限性，公开了框架、策略检查点和完整结果以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [60] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出源接地特征反演方法，通过局部修复反向传播信号（使用Wiener映射），在不需逐查询优化的条件下，将神经网络内部特征逆向映射回输入域，实现层级化特征可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征反演方法仅匹配目标特征，但存在多解性问题，无法确定与生成该特征的特定样本相关联的逆映射，需要一种能保留源样本局部几何信息的反演方法。

Method: 在计算图的每个边界处，利用反向传播提供正确的反向依赖，但修复其传递的伴随信号：首先用闭式矩阵Wiener映射从均值种子VJP估计上游状态，再用第二个Wiener映射处理JVP前向一致性残差，通过一次有限反向遍将修复后的状态沿同一DAG组合。该方法无需逐查询优化，支持多种架构和通道组。

Result: 通过匹配目标和源的控制实验验证了每个逆映射依赖于所选特征和被解释样本的局部算子而非目标无关的图像模板。预测条件特征图谱将可视化结果与对相应内部特征的独立干预进行对齐。

Conclusion: 源接地特征反演能够打开网络隐藏的特征层级结构，在单层和单通道级别进行检验，将网络从输入中提取的内容与塑造其决策的内部证据联系起来。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [61] [A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12550)
*Rahul Krishnan,Volker Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JoLT是一种针对Transformer推理中KV缓存的三阶张量压缩方法，通过部分Tucker分解（仅压缩token和特征轴）加JL旋转低比特残差，在每字节预算下用拉格朗日对偶分配Tucker秩和残差比特位宽，实现近无损2-3x压缩，在Mistral-7B和LLaMA-2-13B上困惑度、GSM8K准确率和RULER检索均保持统计不变，且FlashJoLT变体可提供5-13x压缩加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法（低秩分解和量化）分别处理矩阵或独立条目，忽略了KV缓存作为三阶张量（头轴、token轴、特征轴）各轴冗余度不同的自然结构，导致压缩效率受限。

Method: 将KV缓存视为三阶张量，对token和特征轴施加部分Tucker分解，保留头和层轴不变；用Johnson-Lindenstrauss旋转的低比特残差恢复截断能量；通过拉格朗日对偶在每字节预算下联合优化Tucker秩和残差比特位宽，逐层分组且对K/V分别处理。

Result: 在Mistral-7B和LLaMA-2-13B上实现近无损2-3x压缩，困惑度、GSM8K、RULER均无统计显著下降；2x压缩下K/V相对Frobenius误差分别0.009/0.006，比跨层SVD和4-bit量化低一个数量级；FlashJoLT实现5-13x压缩加速。

Conclusion: 利用张量结构的多轴冗余特征，结合低秩分解与量化残差，可在保持精度的同时大幅压缩KV缓存，为长上下文推理的显存瓶颈提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference. It grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the ceiling on throughput. Two families of methods reduce it. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither family exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT, applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, and then restores the energy that truncation discards with a Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) rotated low-bit residual. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression: perplexity, GSM8K accuracy, and RULER needle-in-a-haystack retrieval all stay at or within statistical noise of the uncompressed baseline on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B). At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures, roughly an order of magnitude below cross-layer SVD and 4-bit quantization. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at matched quality.

</details>


### [62] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多尺度自编码器（LMSAE），利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征，在保持高检测性能的同时大幅降低参数量（模型<500KB），在Jetson Nano上实现9倍推理延迟降低和2倍功耗降低，适用于边缘物联网环境下的单变量时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法在物联网和边缘监控场景中面临计算开销大、资源受限的问题，且缺乏多尺度敏感性，难以捕捉细微异常。

Method: 提出LMSAE网络，采用离散小波变换（DWT）提取多尺度特征，并结合多尺度损失函数增强对细微或隐藏异常的敏感性。

Result: 在基准数据集上以不到500KB的模型大小和显著更少的参数实现具有竞争性或更优的检测性能；在Jetson Nano上推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍。

Conclusion: LMSAE是一种高效、轻量的边缘部署方案，在资源约束条件下实现了时间序列异常检测性能与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>


### [63] [The Geometry of Memorization: Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity as a Diagnostic for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12616)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限时间谱灵敏度(FTSS)指标g(t)，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示连续时间生成框架中的流形几何结构，并发现过拟合会导致谱坍缩现象，据此开发了一种基于内部轨迹动力学的生成记忆检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成框架中，理论目标倾向于直线轨迹，但经验网络会发展出复杂的路径形变。作者希望理解这种几何结构，并利用其检测生成模型中的记忆化（过拟合）问题。

Method: 提出FTSS g(t)指标，这是一种无梯度、前向传递的度量方法，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示流形几何结构，作为稳定秩的连续代理。利用g(t)检测数据稀缺下的谱坍缩现象。

Result: 泛化模型保持稳定的有效维度，而过拟合模型则发生谱坍缩。基于g(t)的内部几何审计可以检测生成记忆化，无需外部成员查询或基线数据比较。

Conclusion: g(t)能够有效揭示生成模型的几何病理，并作为一种纯内部轨迹动力学的方法检测过拟合记忆化，避免了传统方法对外部数据的依赖。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>


### [64] [Learning Forced Multibody Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12627)
*Martine Dyring Hansen,Marta Ghirardelli,Elena Celledoni,David Martin de Diego,Brynjulf Owren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于离散强迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在李群上仅使用位置数据学习机械系统动力学的架构，该方法通过直接在流形值构型空间上建模动力学，自然地尊重系统的几何结构并保持几何不变量和守恒律。仅依赖位置测量使得该方法适用于速度数据不可用或存在噪声的场景，并可扩展到多体系统及外部控制输入，在合成和真实数据集上均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械系统动力学学习方法通常需要速度数据或忽略系统的几何结构，导致在速度数据不可用、含噪或系统具有复杂几何约束时性能受限。本文旨在开发一种仅依赖位置数据且能保持系统几何与物理结构的学习方法。

Method: 基于离散强迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程（Discrete Forced Euler-Lagrange Equations）在李群（Lie Groups）上建模动力学。方法直接在流形值构型空间（manifold-valued configuration spaces）上操作，利用离散几何力学框架，仅使用位置数据作为输入，并自然地扩展到多体系统（multibody systems）和外部控制输入（external control inputs）。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上均展示了强性能，表明该方法能够有效地学习机械系统动力学，同时保持几何不变量和守恒律。

Conclusion: 该架构为学习机械系统动力学提供了一种几何结构保持且仅需位置数据的方法，适用于速度数据缺失或有噪声的实际应用场景，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data. By formulating the dynamics directly on manifold-valued configuration spaces, the method naturally respects the geometric structure of the systems and preserves geometric invariants and conservation laws. The reliance on position measurements alone makes the framework applicable in settings where velocity data are unavailable or noisy. The approach extends naturally to multibody systems, accommodates external control inputs, and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [65] [AdaPCLA: Adaptive Prior-Calibrated Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Longitudinal EHR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12645)
*Shuai Cui,Chen Wenxuan,Wenjie Du,Jian Lou,Dan Li,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaPCLA框架通过数据分布感知的训练策略（模拟退火训练）和零样本分布控制，使生成模型能够自适应地拟合和生成电子健康记录数据，特别改善了对罕见事件（疾病、症状）共现结构的表征，在尾事件合理性、下游任务效用和零样本跨群体适应方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型在处理纵向电子健康记录生成时，倾向于低估罕见事件（如疾病、症状）的共现结构，导致生成数据对罕见亚群体的保真度和忠实度降低。

Method: 提出AdaPCLA框架：1）通过模拟退火训练实现数据分布感知的训练策略，内化数据知识参数；2）支持零样本分布控制，实现无需训练即可适应多样化临床群体的生成；3）通过标签经验神经正切核（NTK）理论分析罕见代码对数更新，导出先验内化界。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，TailPairSeen指标比HALO分别提升114.2%和65.1%；零样本跨群体适应F1比GPT风格生成提升3.5%。

Conclusion: AdaPCLA在尾事件合理性、下游任务效用和零样本控制方面取得一致增益，有效提升了罕见群体的生成质量。

Abstract: Generative modeling of longitudinal Electronic Health Records is increasingly important for privacy-preserving research, yet standard autoregressive models tend to underrepresent the co-occurrence structure of tail events (i.e., diseases, symptoms), reducing the fidelity and faithfulness of generated data for rare subpopulations. To this end, we propose AdaPCLA framework, which enables generative models to adaptively fit and generate EHR data through a data distribution-aware training strategy; this is achieved by internalizing data knowledge parameters by simulated annealing training. It also supports training-free adaptation to a diverse clinical population for generation through zero-shot distribution control. Moreover, our theoretical analysis characterizes rare-code logit updates through the label-wise empirical NTK and derives a prior-internalization bound for how annealing speed and NTK conditioning affect retained prior signals. Experiments on real-world data show that AdaPCLA achieves consistent gains in tail plausibility, downstream utility, and zero-shot control; in particular, it improves TailPairSeen over HALO by 114.2% on MIMIC-III and 65.1% on MIMIC-IV, outperforms GPT-style generation by 3.5% F1 for zero-shot cross-population adaptation.

</details>


### [66] [Extractable Memorization From First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12649)
*A. Feder Cooper,Marika Swanberg,Jamie Hayes,Lea Duesterwald,Christopher De Sa,Daniel E. Ho,Mark A. Lemley,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型中“可提取记忆”的定义和验证方法进行了改进。现有研究存在两种相反的有效性问题：要么高估了提取的证据（将短序列的可预测性误认为记忆），要么低估了提取的有效性（模型也能生成未训练过的真实文本）。作者提出**匹配比较**方法——通过比较训练序列与非训练序列的生成概率基线来判定是否真正存在记忆。具体包括两种形式：(1) 基于共形预测的统计检验，可在指定假阳性率下校准阈值；(2) 针对单一文档（如书籍）的普查方法。实验表明，该方法能实现严格、可校准的记忆性判断，并揭示出过往研究中的假阳性问题，如OLMo在Wikipedia上24%的训练序列生成率实际上是假阳性。对于Llama 3.1在书籍上的记忆序列，校准的阈值低至1e-27，支持在现实采样预算下几乎不可能提取到的序列的记忆性主张。最后，作者重新定义了“可提取记忆”，要求同时满足有效的记忆性主张和在合理预算内近乎确定的生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM可提取记忆的研究存在两类相反的有效性问题：一是高估了提取（将短序列的可预测性误认为记忆），二是低估了提取（模型也能生成未训练过的真实文本）。两者都忽视了记忆性主张的核心标准——模型必须以足够高的概率生成训练序列。作者旨在提出一种严格、可校准的方法来区分真正的记忆与可预测性。

Method: 提出**匹配比较**框架，通过比较训练序列与非训练序列的生成概率来判断记忆性。具体有两种形式：(1) 基于共形预测的统计检验，在指定假阳性率(FPR)下校准阈值；(2) 普查方法，针对单个文档匹配非训练文档进行校准。在Wikipedia和书籍数据集上对OLMo 2 32B和Llama 3.1 70B等模型进行实验验证。

Result: 在Wikipedia上，OLMo 2 32B对非训练10-token后缀的生成率约为训练后缀的24%，表明该比例的训练生成率包含大量假阳性。对Llama 3.1 70B在书籍上的记忆检测，校准的阈值低至1e-27，支持那些在现实采样预算内几乎无法提取的序列仍具有记忆性。

Conclusion: 匹配比较方法能够严格、可校准地判断模型是否记忆了训练数据，揭示过往方法的假阳性问题。作者据此重新定义了“可提取记忆”，要求同时满足有效的记忆性主张和合理预算内近乎确定的生成能力。

Abstract: Recent work on extractable memorization in LLMs suffers from two contrasting validity problems. Some studies overstate extraction, e.g., relying on sequences too short to distinguish memorization from predictability. Others imply that extraction is unreliable evidence of memorization, since models can also reproduce real-world text they weren't explicitly trained on. In different ways, both overlook what makes a valid extraction claim: the model must generate a training sequence with high enough probability to indicate memorization. To determine what's high enough, one has to perform a matched comparison: measuring the generation probabilities of both the training sequences of interest and comparable non-training sequences. Because non-training sequences cannot have been memorized, their probabilities provide a baseline for predictability; a training sequence exceeding this baseline provides evidence of memorization. We formalize matched comparisons in two ways: (1) a conformal test that calibrates a threshold to a chosen FPR when training and non-training sequences are sampled from populations, and (2) a census that calibrates against a matched non-training document when the object is a single document (e.g., a book). We show that matched comparisons enable rigorous, calibrated memorization claims, and reveal where prior setups have validity issues. For instance, on Wikipedia OLMo 2 32B reproduces non-training 10-token suffixes roughly 24% as often as training ones: that share of the training generation rate reflects false positives, not memorization. For Llama 3.1 70B on books, the thresholds we calibrate are as low as 1e-27, supporting memorization claims for sequences that no feasible sampling budget would extract. Based on these results, we refine "extractable memorization" to require a valid memorization claim and near-certain generation within a realistic budget.

</details>


### [67] [Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning: A Path Toward Eliminating LLM Hallucination in Empirical Inference via Tool-Attested Kernel Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12650)
*Junyu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-VAR架构，基于Lean 4形式化验证工具，确保大语言模型（LLM）的推理输出必须源自经过验证的工具调用证据和内核检查的有效推理链，否则输出为可审计的"Abstain"。在TableBench数值推理（n=120）上达到120/120满分，反事实压力测试中保持100%源忠实度，语义形式化错误率仅1.7%-3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM工具调用架构中，仅通过工具访问无法保证经验推理的可控性：被接受的输出未必源自经过证实的证据，被接受的推理未必经得起形式化审查。需要一种技术治理机制，确保高风险经验主张的输出可审计、可追溯。

Method: EG-VAR是一个基于Lean 4的架构，将Lean内核作为"已验证"（Verified）声明的唯一铸造者，通过工具证言公理（tool-attestation axioms）和声明源提升（declared source lifts）实现。所有验证输出在结构上源自经证实的工具调用（定理3.1）和内核检查的有效推理链（定理3.2）；其余输出则是诚实的"Abstain"，附带可重放的审计轨迹。

Result: 在TableBench数值推理子集（n=120）上，EG-VAR达到120/120，而基线（same-tool）为95%；在反事实压力测试（5个领域×2个模型）中，EG-VAR保持100%源忠实度，同工具基线降至80-90%，无工具基线降至50-80%。语义形式化错误率：Sonnet上3.3%，Opus上1.7%。

Conclusion: EG-VAR定位为高风险经验主张的技术治理接口：形式化侧车（formal sidecar）使目标命题、源范围、证据边界、证明义务和弃权条件均可审计，消除了当前无支撑的"已验证"输出，同时将形式化错误、源争议、歧义和弃权转化为明确的审计目标。未来，类型化侧车可在数据集、API、公共记录和AI生成文档中摊销形式化负担。

Abstract: Tool access alone does not make LLM empirical reasoning governable: accepted outputs need not descend from attested evidence, and accepted deductions need not hold up under formal scrutiny. We present EG-VAR (Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning), a Lean 4-based tool-calling architecture in which the Lean kernel is the sole minter of Verified claims via tool-attestation axioms and declared source lifts. Every verified output structurally descends from an attested tool call (Thm. 3.1) and a kernel-checked chain of valid inference (Thm. 3.2); residual outputs are honest Abstain with a replayable audit trail. On a subcollection of TableBench numerical reasoning (n=120), EG-VAR attains 120/120 versus a 95% same-tool baseline; on counterfactual stress tests (5 domains x 2 models), EG-VAR stays 100% source-faithful while same-tool drops to 80-90% (no-tool 50-80%). With the LLM as deployment-time formalizer, residual semantic-formalization error is 3.3% on Sonnet and 1.7% on Opus. We position EG-VAR as a technical-governance interface for high-stakes empirical claims: a formal sidecar makes the target proposition, source scope, evidence boundary, proof obligation, and abstention condition auditable, eliminating unsupported Verified outputs today while turning formalization errors, lift and source-authority disputes, ambiguities, and abstentions into explicit audit targets. Over time, typed sidecars in datasets, APIs, public records, and AI-generated documents can amortize this formalization burden into reusable infrastructure.

</details>


### [68] [Learning-based Probabilistic Load Forecasting with Post-hoc and In-model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12730)
*Sarah Al-Shareeda,Gulcihan Ozdemir,Heung Seok Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能建筑负荷预测中，当推理时输入特征需重建时，不确定性应置于何处。通过对比模块化后验残差分位数与集成式模型内分位数学习两种方案，使用三种深度学习骨干网络（递归、混合递归、注意力机制TFT），发现不确定性放置位置依赖于骨干网络类型。集成式分位数学习在TFT上表现最佳（MAPE 2.2-3.6%，RMSE 28-83W），区间宽度比模块化方案窄约5倍。重建输入会使分位数得分增加106%而区间宽度几乎不变，表明模型无法自动吸收重建带来的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能建筑负荷预测模型通常在密集、多变量、高频数据上离线训练，但部署时可能仅提供小时级、特征有限的输入。缺失特征必须重建，其误差会通过模型传播。若输入不确定性未被反映，预测区间可能校准不良，影响需求响应调度。现有研究缺乏对推理时输入重建场景下不确定性放置位置的系统比较。

Method: 开发统一的一日前概率预测框架，对齐时间分辨率、重建不可用输入并推导因果特征。比较模块化后验残差分位数方案与集成式模型内分位数学习方案。使用三种深度学习骨干网络（循环、混合循环、基于注意力的TFT），在相同输入、预测范围、预处理规则和训练预算下进行比较。通过Diebold-Mariano检验和重建敏感性测试评估。

Result: 不确定性放置位置依赖于骨干网络。集成式分位数学习在TFT上最可靠，MAPE 2.2-3.6%，RMSE 28-83W，区间宽度比模块化方案窄约5倍。重建输入使分位数得分增加106%而区间宽度几乎不变。Diebold-Mariano检验支持TFT排名。与非DL基线和季节性保留周的鲁棒性检验支持该排名。

Conclusion: 后验残差分位数在推理依赖重建输入时存在局限性，模型无法自动吸收重建带来的不确定性。集成式分位数学习与TFT结合是最佳方案。

Abstract: Smart-building load forecasters are often trained offline on dense, multivariate, high-frequency data, but deployment may provide only hourly, feature-limited inputs. Missing features must then be reconstructed, and their errors can propagate through the model. If this input uncertainty is not reflected, prediction intervals may become miscalibrated, affecting demand-response scheduling. Our work examines where uncertainty should be placed once inference inputs are reconstructed. We develop a unified one-day-ahead probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns temporal resolution, reconstructs the unavailable inputs, and derives causal features, and we compare a modular post-hoc residual-quantile scheme with an integrated in-model quantile-learning scheme. The comparison uses three mid-scale Deep Learning (DL) backbones: recurrent, hybrid recurrent, and attention-based Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) models, under identical inputs, forecasting horizon, preprocessing rules, and training budgets. Results show that uncertainty placement is backbone-dependent. Integrated quantile learning is most reliable with the TFT, yielding 2.2-3.6% MAPE and 28-83W RMSE on the labeled test window, while producing intervals about 5x narrower than the modular intervals at the closest-to-nominal coverage level. Diebold-Mariano tests support the TFT ranking and the mixed behavior of the recurrent backbones. A reconstruction-sensitivity test shows that reconstructed inputs increase the Quantile Score (QS) by 106% while interval width remains nearly unchanged, indicating that the model does not automatically absorb reconstruction-induced uncertainty. Robustness checks against non-DL baselines and seasonal hold-out weeks support this ranking. Our results expose the limits of post-hoc residual quantiles when inference depends on reconstructed inputs.

</details>


### [69] [What Makes a Representational Prior Work? Feature Families, Label-Free Invariances, and Critical Windows in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12735)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过188组新实验，系统研究了对比先验在神经网络的"grokking"现象中加速泛化的四个关键维度：特征族对齐、无监督不变性、时序窗口和实验设置的稳健性，发现特征族对齐决定先验是否允许泛化，无标签的不变性先验足以加速，且早期短暂施加即可获得大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作表明了grokking延迟的因果机制是形成任务结构化表征所需的时间，且可通过对比先验注入。本文旨在系统刻画什么样的先验才能有效工作，从四个维度进行深入分析。

Method: 在188组新实验中，沿着四个轴线（内容、监督方式、时序、设置）系统对比不同先验的效果。使用模加法和模乘法任务，控制特征族（如幅度带vs正确特征）、监督信号（有标签对比vs纯置换不变性）、施加窗口（早期、连续、后期）及网络深度和归一化变体。通过泛化成功率、中位加速倍数和权重范数延迟律指数进行量化评估。

Result: (1) 内容：来自错误特征族（幅度带）的先验与随机分区一样阻碍泛化，证实先验作用于电路特征层面；(2) 监督：纯无标签置换不变先验在15/15实验中泛化，中位速度提升2.7倍，优于有标签先验；(3) 时序：仅早期2000轮（预算的4%）施加即可10/10泛化且速度提升2.7倍，优于连续施加；(4) 与权重归一化夹子结合获得最强加速（中位17倍，5/5）；(5) 结构注入使权重范数延迟律指数降低约17倍。

Conclusion: 特征族对齐决定先验能否实现泛化；无标签的不变性内容足以提供加速；短暂的早期窗口即可捕获几乎所有收益；任务在记忆前已泛化则不存在延迟可供控制。

Abstract: Companion work showed the grokking delay is causally the time to form task-structured representations, injectable via a contrastive prior. Here we characterize what makes such a prior work, across four axes, in 188 new runs. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. Supervision: a fully label-free invariance prior -- positives are commuted pairs $(a,b)\sim(b,a)$ only -- generalizes in 15/15 runs at a median $2.7\times$ speedup, more reliably than the label-supervised prior itself ($p=0.038$), and combined with a weight-norm clamp yields the strongest method we test (median $17\times$, 5/5) -- strongest meaning reliably fast: plain cross-entropy with a clamp matches this speed only at the exact critical norm, while the prior keeps it fast across the entire clamp range. Timing: the prior is only needed early -- applied solely during the first 2000 epochs (4% of budget) it generalizes 10/10 at $2.7\times$, beating continuous application (8/10, $1.25\times$) and a duration-matched later window ($2.1\times$). Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [70] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于惩罚的约束感知聚合规则（$w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$），用于联邦强化学习中的分布式能源协调，在不修改本地训练的情况下，相比FedAvg显著降低违规次数并提升奖励，在 DairyGridEnv 及真实负荷数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦强化学习聚合方法（如FedAvg）未考虑系统级约束，导致全局行为不安全。现有方法依赖对偶优化或修改本地训练，复杂度高。需要一种轻量级、无需修改本地训练的约束感知聚合策略，在保持奖励的同时提升安全性。

Method: 提出约束感知聚合规则，服务器端更新时同时考虑局部性能（奖励 $R_i$）与估计的约束违规程度 $V_i$。其中惩罚式规则 $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$ 无需对偶优化或修改本地训练。在 DairyGridEnv（多农场电池储能协调，含共享电网容量约束）及芬兰真实负荷数据和德国FIELD数据集上进行评估。

Result: 惩罚式聚合规则在合成和真实负荷场景下均显著降低违规次数，同时提升奖励。提出的奖励-违规组合方案（$λ$ 可调）暴露了可调控的权衡但稳定性较差。惩罚式规则在多个随机种子下始终提供最可靠的奖励-安全权衡。

Conclusion: 轻量级聚合策略能够在保持标准通信协议的前提下，显著提升联邦强化学习中的经验安全性，无需复杂的对偶优化或本地训练修改。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并基于FukuyamaBench基准测试，通过微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在精确路径匹配上超越专用FlowER模型（8.3% vs 5.1%），证明机理感知训练能显著增强LLM的化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学LLM主要聚焦粗粒度的命名反应预测和逆合成分析，常导致物理不一致性和幻觉；而专用小规模机理推理模型在不同化学空间中的泛化能力受限。为此，需要一种既能保持语言模型强大泛化性又能进行精确逐步骤机理推理的方法。

Method: 1) 构建大规模反应机理推理数据集；2) 建立FukuyamaBench分层机理推理基准；3) 基于Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行机理感知微调。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3-30B-A3B在FukuyamaBench Set A上达到8.3%精确路径匹配，超越专用FlowER模型的5.1%。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能够显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力，在反应机理推断任务上优于专用小规模生成模型。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [72] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AVQ Attention通过自适应分配码本容量，在保持O(MN)复杂度的同时，实现对高注意力区域精细量化、低注意力区域粗量化的改进，相比固定码本VQ注意力取得了更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的O(N²)计算复杂度是Transformer模型的瓶颈。VQ注意力虽将其降至O(MN)，但码本容量均匀分配，导致高注意力区域可能被粗粒度近似，而低注意力区域浪费表示能力。

Method: 提出自适应向量量化注意力(AVQ Attention)，从少量码字开始，在前向传播中识别最重要的码字并用预学习的子码字进行细化，实现高注意力区域的细粒度量化。通过自定义Triton内核，在Flash Attention的平铺计算范式内完成重要性评分、子码字插入和父贡献替换等操作。

Result: 在保持O(MN)复杂度的前提下，相比固定码本VQ注意力取得了更好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AVQ Attention通过自适应码本分配策略，有效解决了VQ注意力中码本容量分配不均的问题，实现了更优的性能。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [73] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述论文提出了一个统一的延迟分解框架，用于分析扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率，并从算法、架构/系统和推理时扩展三个维度系统梳理了加速技术，最后给出了可复现基准测试的指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽具有并行生成的理论优势，但并行生成本身并不保证实际加速。实际部署中，推理效率是关键前提，而端到端延迟受到算法、架构和系统层面因素之间复杂权衡的影响，现有基准测试往往混淆了这些因素，导致严格比较困难。

Method: 提出一个统一的延迟分解框架，将dLLM推理延迟拆解为算法、架构和系统三个层面的独立因素进行分析；基于此框架，从算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展三个维度对现有加速技术进行分类和梳理；并提供可复现基准测试的指导原则。

Result: 建立了dLLM推理延迟的统一分解框架，系统分类了算法、架构/系统和推理时扩展三个维度的加速技术，识别了现有基准测试中混淆不同层面因素的弊端。

Conclusion: 并行生成的实际加速需要扩散感知的缓存和复用等专门推理机制；延迟分解框架有助于厘清不同层面因素对推理速度的影响；该领域仍面临开放挑战，充分发挥并行生成潜力需要进一步的研究。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [74] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）微调开源推理模型，使其能够根据文本状态和预测输出热泵设定点，实现建筑热储能调度。仅用30条训练提示，模型碳排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近动态规划最优值60.8 kg-CO2。推理能力对性能至关重要，微调主要稳定了已有规划模式而非创造新策略，且方法可泛化至电池任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑需要根据电网条件调整冷却负荷，热储能（TES）可以实现这一调整，但调度需提前数小时规划且受存储约束。现有的模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习方法难以跨建筑规模化部署，因此需要一种更可扩展且无需大量任务特定训练的调度方法。

Method: 将精确离线动态规划（DP）的动作值转换为密集奖励信号，通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源推理模型进行微调。模型作为上层调度器，从文本状态和预测中输出每小时热泵设定点。仅使用30条训练提示进行强化微调（RFT），并在一个简单的办公建筑TES基准上进行评估。

Result: RFT将开源模型的碳排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近DP最优值60.8 kg-CO2。GPT-5无需任务特定训练即可接近DP和MPC性能，而GPT-4o（非推理LLM）碳排放高于无存储基线。RFT主要稳定了候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查等规划模式，而非创造新策略。方法在预测误差和未见过的TES条件下保持有效，并泛化至电池任务。

Conclusion: 基于DP的可验证奖励为将开源推理模型适配到建筑存储调度提供了实用途径。推理能力对于调度性能至关重要，该方法具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为更高保真度的全建筑控制和城域能源管理可扩展验证器研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [75] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，用于解决张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测问题。该框架将总势能最小化和本构关系嵌入训练目标，同时预测平衡节点构型及杆件力、力密度等物理量。数值实验表明该方法具有良好的物理一致性、鲁棒性、数据效率以及可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体结构的找形与物理属性预测是结构力学中的基本逆问题，但由于几何与力的强非线性耦合、结构稳定性保障以及边界条件和对称性等约束的施加，传统方法求解困难且对噪声和异常值缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出基于能量的学习框架，将总势能最小化和本构关系作为物理损失直接融入训练目标，实现同时预测平衡节点构型及相关物理量（杆件力、力密度）。

Result: 在棱柱和着陆器两种张拉整体结构上的数值实验表明，该方法具有良好的物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率，具备可扩展的找形能力与准确的结构属性预测能力。

Conclusion: 基于能量的物理损失嵌入学习过程可以有效提升物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率，为解决张拉整体结构的找形与物理属性预测问题提供了一种有前景的方案。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [76] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过鼓励类内坍塌和类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表示，在OpenML-CC18基准数据集上取得了与SOTA方法（包括核SVM、随机森林、点回归和交叉熵神经网络）相竞争的性能，并具有更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数（如交叉熵和点回归）在学习归一化且结构良好的表示方面存在不足，初始化离最优配置较远、梯度信息不足、对类内坍塌的激励不够强，需要一种新的损失函数来同时实现类内紧凑和类间可分离。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，同时鼓励类内坍塌（intra-class collapse）和类间对比（inter-class contrast），允许神经网络保持足够灵活性以逼近几何最优的嵌入表示，实现类别间大角度分离。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多种表格数据集上，CoCo损失取得了与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和交叉熵神经网络等SOTA方法相竞争的性能；理论和实验均表明该损失促进了更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CoCo损失是一种有效的目标函数，能够学习判别性表示同时保持有竞争力的预测性能，兼具类内坍塌和类间对比的优点。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [77] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效随机预测器，将SPR-Calibration过程与外部Blackwell式校正层结合，将在线二元序列校准问题的期望校准误差从经典的T^(2/3)下界突破至O(T^(2/3-ε))，其中ε>0为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破在线校准问题中经典的T^(2/3)校准误差下界屏障，利用Dagan等人(2024)的最新成果，进一步降低期望校准误差的阶数。

Method: 将SPR-Calibration过程（用于控制相对于替代条件均值估计序列的校准误差）与外部Blackwell式校正层（用于控制使用替代序列近似真实结果时产生的额外误差）相结合。总校准误差分解为替代校准误差和替代序列与真实结果之间的残差，前者由SPR-Calibration保证界定，后者通过二次势能论证结合SPR-Calibration预测器的稀疏性来控制。

Result: 实现了预期的校准误差为O(T^(2/3-ε))，其中ε>0为某常数，优于经典的T^(2/3)阶。

Conclusion: 通过双层结构设计（SPR-Calibration + Blackwell校正层）有效降低了在线二元序列校准问题的误差上界，展示了超越经典下界的可行性。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [78] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于频谱的可预测性指标无法衡量上下文（长回溯、检索增强、预训练模型）带来的超越二阶统计量的价值，并提出覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit）作为新的诊断指标来区分频谱内与频谱外的预测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用频谱基可预测性指标来回答"添加上下文是否有帮助"这一不同问题，但两者并非同一问题。频谱指标对相位随机化不变，而超越二阶的价值（如检索和基础模型提供的）则不然，因为相位随机化后的序列渐近高斯。需要明确区分并给出诊断工具。

Method: 提出不可能性结果：任何基于功率谱的指标在相位随机化下不变。构建保持频谱和边际的替代对（surrogate pairs）进行隔离对比。给出无标签、配置级诊断——覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit），其主项通过模拟预测与线性预测的增益比衡量超越频谱的结构。

Result: 在7个基准上验证：窗口键控检索的价值在替代对中崩塌（ECL中位数+33%→-35%，p<10^-40），而所有频谱指标保持不变；基础模型的价值分裂为存活的二阶部分和崩塌的超越线性部分；更长线性窗口的价值存活。留一数据集验证中，结构项能预测超越频谱价值的符号，而频谱指标则相反。

Conclusion: 贡献在于区分了"序列可预测性"与"上下文价值"两个问题，提供了受控比较和部署决策的诊断工具。不引入新预测模型。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [79] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero 是一个程序化驾驶模拟器和自博弈训练框架，在单一服务器级 GPU 上实现每秒 130 万智能体步数的运行速度，无需人类演示即可从头训练出零样本跨城市泛化的驾驶策略，首个完全学习型策略在 InterPlan 长尾基准测试中登顶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模拟器难以同时满足强化学习所需的快速性、基于真实地图结构的现实性以及覆盖安全关键长尾场景的多样性。基于日志数据的方法无法提供足够广泛的场景覆盖。

Method: 基于可配置 C 引擎，采用 CPU 模拟与 GPU 策略推理的零拷贝路径实现高性能；利用真实地图几何数据填充随机化规则驱动交通参与者；通过随机化智能体动力学、奖励和配置实现无限场景生成；采用纯强化学习自博弈训练方案，无人类演示和回退规划器。

Result: 1.3M 智能体步数/秒（单 GPU）；InterPlan 长尾基准测试登顶（首个完全学习策略）；val14 基准中碰撞和时间-碰撞得分最优（最安全）；Waymo Open Sim Agents  realism 基准中超越其他无演示方法，媲美最强参考锚定自博弈方法。

Conclusion: TerraZero 证明纯强化学习自博弈方案无需人类演示即可生成跨城市零样本泛化的驾驶策略，且能同时服务于驾驶策略和仿真智能体两种角色，在安全性和长尾场景覆盖方面达到了领先水平。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [80] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在处理多球碰撞等需要序列推理的任务时存在"序列性差距"——其去噪循环无法提供可扩展的序列计算能力，导致随着因果链增长性能下降，而自回归/分块生成和增加网络深度能有效缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标准双向视频扩散模型在多球硬球动力学等因果链较长的序列推理任务中的表现，揭示其性能瓶颈及其根本原因。

Method: 通过控制实验对比多球（存在球间交互）与单球（无交互）动力学场景，进行干预研究（自回归/分块生成、增加网络深度），并从理论上证明确定性视频预测中去噪步骤不增加序列计算量。

Result: 多球场景下性能随因果链增长而下降，单球控制实验中没有该退化现象；增加有效序列计算的方法（自回归生成、更深的网络）能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型存在"序列性差距"，即任务所需的序列计算量与模型去噪循环提供的序列计算能力不匹配，这是其处理序列推理和模拟任务的结构性障碍。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [HARP-ME: Closure-Driven Exact Induced Motif Enumeration on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12074)
*Ashwina Kumar,Rupesh Nasre*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HARP-ME是一个GPU框架，用于精确枚举连接的四节点诱导子图。它通过闭包感知编译和诱导签名重用技术，减少候选扩展和重复边检查，在多种图上比现有方法快1.83-10.73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU上的精确诱导子图枚举因候选扩展不规则、重复集合交集和执行计数需同时考虑边的存在与否而具有挑战性，现有方法效率不高。

Method: 提出了闭包感知编译（基于遍历代价、代数推导收益、状态重用和分区开销选择锚点）和诱导签名重用（利用候选前沿信息和紧凑约束识别可重用的完成状态），并采用规范锚点所有者规则处理超GPU内存图。

Result: 在六个图上，HARP-ME比Pangolin快2.11倍，比分区PBE快1.83倍，比CPU基线快10.73倍，缓存命中率64%-76%，主机到设备数据传输开销显著低于PBE风格分区。

Conclusion: 优化锚点选择以获取代数推导收益可以互补基于遍历和重用的GPU子图枚举技术。

Abstract: Exact induced motif enumeration is a fundamental operation in graph mining, but it remains challenging on GPUs because candidate expansion is irregular, repeated set intersections dominate execution, and induced counting must consider both the presence and absence of edges. We present HARP-ME, which stands for Hierarchical Anchor-Reuse Partitioned Motif Enumeration. It is a GPU framework for the exact enumeration of connected induced four-node motifs. HARP-ME introduces closure-aware compilation, which selects a set of anchors for explicit enumeration by considering traversal cost, algebraic derivation benefits, expected state reuse, and the additional halo overhead caused by graph partitioning. HARP-ME also introduces induced-signature reuse. This technique identifies reusable completion states using candidate-frontier information together with compact constraints representing both adjacency and non-adjacency relationships. For graphs that exceed GPU memory, a canonical anchor-owner rule ensures exact counting across partitions with overlapping halo regions. We do not claim that the individual graphlet closure identities are new. Instead, our contribution is their integration into a GPU execution framework that reduces both explicit candidate expansion and repeated induced-edge checks. Across six social, web, biological, and synthetic graphs, HARP-ME is the fastest among the evaluated methods. It achieves up to 2.11 times speedup over Pangolin, up to 1.83 times speedup over partitioned PBE, and up to 10.73 times speedup over the evaluated CPU baseline. Detailed measurements show cache-hit rates between 64% and 76%, along with substantially lower host-to-device data transfer overhead than PBE-style partitioning. These results demonstrate that optimizing anchor selection for algebraic derivation benefits can complement traversal-based and reuse-based GPU motif enumeration techniques.

</details>


### [82] [Toward Trustworthy Autonomous Science: A Two-Year Community Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12113)
*Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Milad Abolhasani,Peter Beaucage,Laura Biven,Michael Bussmann,Kyle Chard,Ryan Coffee,Stephen DeWitt,Sagar Dolas,Carrie Eckert,David Elbert,Ian Foster,Tirthankar Ghosal,Anna Giannakou,Tom Gibbs,Leslie Hamilton,Glenn Lockwood,Theresa Mayer,Ben Mintz,Raffi Nazikian,Sal Nimer,Amanda Randles,Woong Shin,Sreenivas Rangan Sukumar,Frédéric Suter,Mitra Taheri,Michela Taufer,Draguna Vrabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文更新了AISLE自主科学实验室路线图，指出领域进展快于预期但面临验证瓶颈——产生候选发现已非难点，验证才是关键限制因素。将路线图从五维度扩展至七维度，新增信任/验证/可重复性和安全/安全/治理两个维度，评估了原有14个里程碑并新增4个里程碑，规划了两年发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学实验室领域虽进展迅速（多智能体系统、自驱动实验室互操作性提升、基础模型能力增强、美国联邦科学战略将自主实验置于核心），但出现了标志性发现被更正、基准测试显示模型在开放式研究任务中表现远逊于封闭式问题、伪造引用等严重问题，产生了"产生候选发现容易，验证困难"的核心张力，亟需更新路线图以应对这一不对称性。

Method: 基于原始AISLE路线图的五维度框架（自主实验室互操作性），更新为七维度：保留原有五维度并将"信任、验证与可重复性"及"安全、安全与治理"从交叉关注提升为第一类维度。评估原始14个里程碑（M1-M14）状态（已达成、部分达成、重新定义、未完成），新增4个里程碑（M15-M18）。规划两年时间线：第一年聚焦接口、协议采纳和验证基础设施建设，第二年聚焦联邦化、零信任协调和治理。

Result: 识别出领域核心张力从"能力不足"转向"验证瓶颈"；更新了路线图框架（五→七维度）；评估了14个原始里程碑并新增4个；提出了分阶段两年发展路径；强调基层网络作为连接国家项目、国际倡议和商业平台的互操作性基础设施。

Conclusion: 自主科学实验室领域已进入新阶段，验证而非能力成为主要瓶颈。未来方向应围绕信任验证可重复性以及安全治理两个新增维度展开，通过分阶段推进（第一年基础设施，第二年联邦化与治理）实现基层网络的互联互通，避免重新孤岛化。

Abstract: One year ago, the AISLE roadmap argued that autonomous laboratories operated as isolated islands and proposed a grassroots network organized around five critical dimensions. The field has since moved faster than anticipated. Multi-agent systems have produced experimentally validated hypotheses, self-driving laboratories have grown more interoperable and orchestrated, reasoning-trained and domain foundation models have raised the capability ceiling, and the Genesis Mission has placed autonomous experimentation at the center of U.S. federal science strategy, with industry emerging as a primary actor. Progress has met a sobering counter-current, including a corrected flagship discovery result, benchmarks showing that agents which rival experts on closed-ended questions still complete only a fraction of open-ended research, and fabricated citations surfacing at leading venues. We read this as the defining tension of the field. Producing a candidate discovery is no longer the hard part, but verifying it is, and this asymmetry now limits autonomous science more than raw model capability. We update the roadmap around seven dimensions, revisiting the original five and elevating two former cross-cutting concerns, trust, verification, and reproducibility, and safety, security, and governance, to first-class status. We assess the original milestones (M1 through M14) as achieved, partially achieved, reframed, or open, add four new milestones (M15 through M18), and scope the path forward to a two-year horizon. The first year concentrates on interfaces, protocol adoption, and the scaffolding of verification, and the second targets federation, zero-trust coordination, and governance. Throughout, we position the grassroots network as the interoperability fabric that lets national programs, international initiatives, and commercial platforms connect rather than re-silo.

</details>


### [83] [FlashDiff: Efficient Regional Execution and Scheduling for Diffusion Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12121)
*Yaqi Qiao,Ping He,Songrun Xie,Ayush Barik,Chensong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashDiff是一个面向扩散模型的高效推理服务系统，通过自适应区域执行和调度，利用扩散过程中不同潜在区域收敛速度不均以及相邻去噪步之间的时间相关性，选择性执行需要进一步细化的区域，并将释放的计算资源重新分配给并发的服务请求。实验表明，FlashDiff在不同模态任务上可降低30-97%的端到端服务延迟，并提升1.2-2.2倍的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的高效服务面临挑战：与自回归解码不同，扩散推理需要在多个去噪步上反复更新高维潜在表示（全区域执行模式），导致生成延迟高、服务吞吐量受限。现有的多GPU并行方法虽能减少每步计算，但引入大量激活交换开销，通信成本甚至抵消并行收益。

Method: FlashDiff包含三个机制：(1) 利用早期注意力信号将潜在表示分解为连贯的执行区域，保留语义结构并暴露细粒度并行性；(2) 轻量级运行时控制器估计区域活跃度，当进一步细化不太可能影响输出质量时，跳过低影响更新；(3) 亲和性感知的在线调度器，共置依赖区域、平衡GPU间负载，并重用释放的计算容量以提升服务效率。

Result: 在真实世界的图像、视频和音频任务上，FlashDiff将端到端服务延迟降低30-97%，吞吐量提升1.2-2.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过自适应区域执行和调度，FlashDiff有效解决了扩散模型推理中计算效率低下的问题，证明了利用扩散过程非均匀性来优化服务的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the central backbone for modern image, video, and audio generation, but their efficient service remains a challenge. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion inference repeatedly updates high-dimensional spatial or temporal latents over many denoising steps. This all-region execution pattern makes generation latency high and limits serving throughput. Existing multi-GPU parallelization methods can reduce per-step computation, but often introduce substantial activation exchange overhead, causing communication to offset or even outweigh the benefits of parallel execution.
  This paper presents FlashDiff, a diffusion serving system that improves inference efficiency through adaptive regional execution and scheduling. FlashDiff is based on the observation that diffusion refinement is not uniform across latent regions or denoising steps: different regions often stabilize at different rates, while neighboring steps exhibit strong temporal correlation. FlashDiff leverages these properties to selectively execute only regions that require further refinement and to reallocate the resulting compute slack across concurrent serving requests. FlashDiff consists of three mechanisms. First, it decomposes the latent representation into coherent execution regions using early-stage attention signals, preserving semantic structure while exposing fine-grained parallelism. Second, it uses a lightweight runtime controller to estimate region activity and bypass low-impact updates when further refinement is unlikely to affect output quality. Third, it applies an affinity-aware online scheduler that co-locates dependent regions, balances residual load across GPUs, and reuses reclaimed compute capacity to improve serving efficiency. Across real-world image, video, and audio workloads, FlashDiff reduces end-to-end serving latency by 30-97% and improves throughput by 1.2-2.2x.

</details>


### [84] [Decentralized Gradient Descent: Bottleneck Regimes and Budget Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12172)
*Nicolò Michelusi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从资源感知角度研究去中心化梯度下降（DGD）算法，提出了瓶颈中心框架，通过引入梯度多样性-网络连接比（DNR）和梯度-通信噪声比（GCR）两个关键量，刻画了不同误差尺度下的优化瓶颈，并给出了达到目标精度所需的预算-复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DGD的收敛性质已得到充分研究，但达到指定精度所需的通信和计算资源尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，从资源感知视角系统分析DGD的资源需求与预算分配。

Method: 采用多阶段分析方法，引入梯度多样性-网络连接比（DNR）和梯度-通信噪声比（GCR）两个核心量，识别初始化、目标异质性/网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声四个主导阶段，推导最优步长选择和显式预算-复杂度界。

Result: 得到了显式的预算-复杂度界，揭示了总预算可分解为各连续瓶颈阶段的贡献，量化了目标异质性、网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声之间的基本权衡关系。

Conclusion: DNR和GCR决定了优化过程中的瓶颈序列及对应的预算最优策略，为资源受限环境下的分布式优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Decentralized gradient descent (DGD) is widely used for solving distributed optimization problems over networks of agents. While its convergence properties are well understood, less is known about the communication and computation resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. In this paper, we study DGD from a resource-aware perspective and characterize the communication-computation budget required to attain a target error level. We develop a bottleneck-centric framework in which different factors dominate the optimization dynamics at different error scales. Specifically, we identify operating regimes governed by initialization, objective heterogeneity and network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise. To capture these effects, we introduce two fundamental quantities: the gradient-Diversity-to-Network-connectivity Ratio (DNR) and the Gradient-to-Communication-noise Ratio (GCR). We show that these quantities determine the sequence of bottlenecks encountered during optimization and the corresponding budget-optimal operating strategy. Using a multi-stage analysis, we derive optimal stepsize selections and explicit budget-complexity bounds that quantify the budget resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. The resulting expressions reveal how the overall budget decomposes into contributions associated with successive bottlenecks and provide insight into the fundamental tradeoffs among objective heterogeneity, network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise.

</details>


### [85] [Overcoming Orchestration Bottlenecks at Exascale: A Decentralized, Policy-Driven Approach for Sim-AI Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12211)
*Harikrishna Tummalapalli,Christine M. Simpson,Riccardo Balin,Vitali A. Morozov,Thang D. Pham,Murat Keceli,Thomas D. Uram*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种递归层次化的工作流编排系统EnsembleLauncher，采用完全去中心化控制平面和可编程调度策略接口，在Aurora超级计算机上成功扩展到整个机器，可处理高达八百万个串行任务，性能超越现有工具四倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正从单体应用转向耦合仿真-AI工作流，包含高度异构的任务。随着工作流扩展到领先级系统，极端集成规模和任务变异性造成了编排瓶颈。系统级调度器吞吐量有限，工作流工具因刚性控制平面拓扑和静态调度启发式算法面临可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出EnsembleLauncher，一种递归层次化的工作流编排系统，采用完全去中心化的控制平面和可编程调度策略接口。

Result: 在Aurora超级计算机上成功扩展到整个机器，可处理多达八百万个串行任务，性能超出最先进工具四倍以上。可编程调度接口在高方差集成和主动学习管道中显著影响资源利用率。

Conclusion: EnsembleLauncher通过去中心化控制平面和可编程调度策略，有效解决了大规模耦合仿真-AI工作流在极大规模系统中的编排瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Scientific computing is increasingly shifting from monolithic applications to coupled simulation-AI workflows composed of highly heterogeneous tasks with diverse hardware, scale, and runtime requirements. As these workflows scale to leadership-class systems, the resulting extreme ensemble sizes and task variability can create orchestration bottlenecks. System-level schedulers are often configured for limited throughput, while workflow tools face scalability issues due to rigid control-plane topologies and static scheduling heuristics. We introduce EnsembleLauncher, a recursively hierarchical workflow orchestrator for exascale systems, featuring a fully decentralized control plane and a programmable scheduling policy interface. On the Aurora supercomputer, EnsembleLauncher successfully scales to the entire machine with up to eight million serial tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art tools by more than four times. Additionally, we implement a programmable scheduling interface and demonstrate a significant impact of scheduling policies on resource utilization for high-variance ensembles and active learning pipelines representative of modern coupled simulation-AI workflows.

</details>


### [86] [Profiling and Scheduling Complex O-RAN Applications Across the 5G Edge and Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12230)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: O-DAG是一个端到端框架，通过DagProfiler工具、三参数三层网络拓扑、SAGA调度扩展和MintEDGE模拟器，实现了O-RAN中AI/ML流水线（DAG）的执行成本分析、调度映射和5G环境下的验证，发现HEFT算法在所有配置下最优，但调度器排名依赖于工作负载特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN将智能RAN控制分解为相互依赖的AI/ML函数流水线（如流量预测、信号质量估计、切片调度），这些流水线需在分散的远边缘、近边缘和云资源上执行，受异构延迟和带宽约束。尽管可自然表达为有向无环图（DAG），但目前缺乏集成方法来分析其执行成本、通过调度启发式映射到分散基础设施，并在5G蜂窝条件下验证放置结果。

Method: 提出O-DAG框架，包含四个紧密耦合阶段：(1) DagProfiler：开源工具，对O-RAN切片调度器进行插桩，提取每任务指令计数和每边通信量；(2) 参数化三层网络拓扑，编码DU/RIC（远边缘）、边缘（近边缘）和云节点，配备现实链路带宽；(3) SAGA调度框架扩展；(4) 基于MintEDGE模拟器的自定义DAG仿真模块。评估了HEFT、MCT、MinMin、MaxMin、Duplex五种调度算法，配置覆盖5K-50K UE、2-20小区、2-10网络切片。

Result: HEFT在所有配置下均取得最低makespan，但调度器排名依赖工作负载。SAGA-仿真间隙可作为状态诊断工具：负间隙（最高-1.72%）标识计算主导配置（HEFT保守高估），正间隙（+0.64%）在低切片数时暴露通信受限状态（带宽争用超出调度模型假设）。

Conclusion: O-DAG填补了O-RAN AI/ML流水线端到端分析、调度与验证的空白，为5G环境下DAG调度提供了完整的profiling-建模-调度-仿真工具链，所有工件已开源。

Abstract: The O-RAN paradigm decomposes intelligent RAN control into pipelines of interdependent AI/ML functions, including traffic prediction, signal quality estimation, and slice scheduling, that must execute across a dispersed continuum of far-edge, near-edge, and cloud resources under heterogeneous latency and bandwidth constraints. Despite the natural expression of these pipelines as Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs), no integrated methodology exists to profile their execution costs, map them onto dispersed infrastructure via scheduling heuristics, and validate the resulting placement under 5G cellular conditions. We present O-DAG, an end-to-end framework that closes this gap through four tightly coupled stages: (1) DagProfiler, a new open-source tool that instruments O-RAN Slice Scheduler and extracts per-task instruction counts and per-edge communication volumes; (2) a parameterized three-tier network topology encoding far-edge (DU, RIC), near-edge (edge), and cloud nodes with realistic link bandwidths; (3) an extension of the SAGA scheduling framework and (4) a custom DAG simulation module built on the MintEDGE simulator. We evaluate five scheduling algorithms (HEFT, MCT, MinMin, MaxMin, Duplex) for a slice scheduling application across various configurations spanning 5K--50K UEs, 2--20 cells, and 2--10 network slices. HEFT achieves the lowest makespan in all configurations, but scheduler rankings are workload-dependent. The SAGA--simulation gap serves as a regime diagnostic: negative gaps (up to -1.72%) identify compute-dominated configurations where HEFT overestimates conservatively, while a positive gap (+0.64%) at low slice counts exposes a communication-bound regime where bandwidth contention exceeds the scheduling model's assumptions. All artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [87] [Parallelizing Legacy Mesh Generation Software: Lessons Learned from a Pseudo-Constrained Parallel Data Refinement Approach for Advancing Front Local Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12253)
*Kevin Garner,David Marcum,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文总结了将遗留软件AFLR（前沿局部重连）作为黑盒进行并行化的经验。采用数据分解方案和运行时负载均衡系统，在16核上实现约11倍加速，但并行方法的稳定性和可扩展性受限于黑盒的输入边界约束，导致输出网格体积与串行方法不同。结论认为黑盒并行化遗留代码可能不实用，建议采用原本无此类约束的设计来高效利用大规模架构的并发性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将遗留软件AFLR（前沿局部重连）作为黑盒进行并行化，以提升网格细化操作的效率。

Method: 采用数据分解方案，将每个子域使用串行网格生成代码并行细化，并利用运行时系统进行工作负载均衡。

Result: 并行方法在网格细化操作中稳定性良好，使用16个CPU核心时比串行AFLR快约11倍。但完全稳定性和潜在可扩展性受限于黑盒的输入边界约束。

Conclusion: 黑盒并行化遗留代码如AFLR可能不实用，建议采用原本无此类约束的设计来高效利用大规模架构的并发性。

Abstract: This paper presents lessons learned from parallelizing the legacy software known as Advancing Front Local Reconnection (AFLR) as a black box. The parallel procedure utilizes (i) a data decomposition scheme where each subdomain is refined in parallel using the sequential mesh generation code and (ii) a runtime system for work-load balancing. Results on the mesh refinement operation show that the parallel method's stability (output mesh quality) is good and that the parallel method outperforms the serial AFLR by about 11 times when utilizing 16 CPU cores. However, full stability (i.e., generating the same quality as the serial method) and potential scalability is limited due to the constraints set by the black box's input boundary requirement. Satisfying this requirement for each subdomain not only adds overhead but also causes the parallel method to generate a different output mesh volume than that generated by the serial method. The complexity of such a state-of-the-art code requires that it be modified to a non-trivial extent in order to remove these constraints. These results suggest that the parallelization of black-box legacy codes like AFLR may not be practical and instead encourages an approach that is originally designed without such constraints to efficiently leverage the concurrency offered by large-scale architectures.

</details>


### [88] [Scaling Synthetic-Image Pre-Training for Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12583)
*Qianpiao Ma,Xiaozhu Song,Junlong Zhou,Yue Zeng,Jianchun Liu,Huaqing Tu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FeDiSyn是一个统一框架，通过联合优化预训练和联邦微调（FedFT），利用缩放定律确定最优合成图像数量、扩散模型生成设备特定分布的合成数据，以及贡献感知的LoRA配置与带宽分配，在资源受限、系统异构和非独立同分布数据环境下，将大视觉模型训练完成时间减少52.5%以上，通信成本降低97.2%以上，同时保持与现有最优方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FedFT方法面临资源限制、系统异构和非独立同分布数据三大挑战，但现有方案存在不足：合成图像生成方法无法捕获设备特定的特征分布，基于PEFT的FedFT方法常低估弱设备的价值，且预训练与FedFT的优化相互割裂，缺乏整体视角来最大化训练效率。

Method: FeDiSyn提出三个核心组件：(i) FedFT预训练的缩放定律，用于确定最优合成图像数量，平衡预训练收益与生成/预训练成本；(ii) 基于扩散模型的合成图像生成方法，捕获设备特定的特征分布以应对非独立同分布数据；(iii) 贡献感知的LoRA配置与带宽分配算法，确保有效利用信息丰富的设备同时应对系统异构性。

Result: 在真实测试平台上的实验结果表明，FeDiSyn相比现有最优方案，完成时间降低52.5%以上，通信成本降低97.2%以上，同时达到可比的模型精度。

Conclusion: FeDiSyn通过统一框架整体考虑预训练与FedFT的交互，有效解决了资源约束、系统异构和非独立同分布数据三大挑战，显著提升了分布式环境下大视觉模型的训练效率。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning (FedFT) enables adapting pre-trained large vision models (LVMs) on distributed, privacy-sensitive devices, while its practical deployment is hindered by three critical challenges: resource constraints, system heterogeneity, and non-IID data. While prior studies partially address these issues, e.g., by pre-training initial models on synthetic images to mitigate the adverse effects of non-IID data, or leveraging parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to reduce resource consumption, they remain inadequate and fragmented. Specifically, existing synthetic image generation methods fail to capture device-specific feature distributions, while current PEFT-based FedFT methods often undervalue weaker devices that may provide critical information. More importantly, the separate optimization of pre-training and FedFT neglects their inherent connection, lacking a holistic perspective to maximize training efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose FeDiSyn, a unified framework that holistically considers the interplay between pre-training and FedFT to minimize the overall LVM training time. Specifically, FeDiSyn introduces: (i) a scaling law for FedFT pre-training to determine the optimal number of synthetic images, balancing pre-training benefit against generation/pre-training cost, (ii) diffusion-based synthetic image generation that captures device-specific feature distributions for pre-training to tackle non-IID data, and (iii) a contribution-aware LoRA configuration and bandwidth allocation algorithm for FedFT to ensure that informative devices are effectively utilized while addressing system heterogeneity. Experimental results on the real-world testbed demonstrate that FeDiSyn reduces completion time by over 52.5% and communication cost by over 97.2%, while achieving comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [89] [HeteroMosaic: Exposing and Exploiting Heterogeneous Execution Opportunities for Energy-Efficient Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12839)
*Gregory Hyegang Jun,Wesley Pang,Eddie Richter,Mehdi Saeedi,Aporva Amarnath,Pallavi Ferrao,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HeteroMosaic 是一种面向边缘 SoC（集成 CPU、iGPU 和 NPU）的异构优先 LLM 推理调度框架。它通过异构 roofline 模型、微批次分解和踪迹引导的协同优化，在统一内存平台上实现跨加速器的高效调度，在 AMD Ryzen AI 平台上相比 iGPU/NPU 基线及 llama.cpp 等框架获得最高 2.05 倍加速，并降低能耗最多 45.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 运行时在边缘 SoC（集成 CPU、iGPU 和 NPU）上通常仅做粗粒度的设备级决策或孤立地优化单个算子，未能充分利用异构计算资源，尤其是在统一内存平台上，性能同时依赖于设备放置和任务图协调。

Method: 1) 异构 roofline 模型：判断何时联合使用 iGPU 和 NPU 执行有益；2) 将推理分解为保持依赖关系的微批次（micro-batches），暴露跨加速器重叠机会；3) 踪迹引导的调度与设备分配协同优化，考虑内存争用、DVFS、设备差异和 NPU 运行时开销等实际因素。

Result: 在 AMD Ryzen AI 平台的三个设计（NPU 重型、均衡、iGPU 重型）上，均衡平台相比 iGPU 基线加速 1.73 倍，相比 NPU 基线加速 1.78 倍，相比 llama.cpp 等框架加速 2.05 倍，能耗降低最多 45.3%，相比此前异构边缘 AI 方案性能提升最高 2.35 倍。

Conclusion: HeteroMosaic 证明了在统一内存的边缘 SoC 上，通过异构感知的细粒度调度与协同优化，可以显著超越单一加速器或现有异构方案，并有效降低能耗。

Abstract: Modern edge system-on-chips (SoCs) combine CPUs, integrated GPUs (iGPUs), and neural processing units (NPUs), yet existing LLM runtimes typically make coarse device-level decisions or optimize operators in isolation. As a result, they underutilize heterogeneous resources, particularly on unified-memory platforms where performance depends on both device placement and task-graph coordination.
  We present HeteroMosaic, a heterogeneity-first scheduling framework for edge LLM inference. HeteroMosaic first uses a heterogeneous roofline model to identify when combining iGPU and NPU execution is beneficial. It then decomposes inference into dependency-preserving micro-batches that expose cross-accelerator overlap and applies trace-guided co-optimization of scheduling and device allocation under practical effects such as memory contention, DVFS, device variation, and NPU runtime overheads.
  We implement HeteroMosaic in PyTorch C++ and evaluate it on three AMD Ryzen AI platforms spanning NPU-heavy, balanced, and iGPU-heavy designs. On the balanced platform, HeteroMosaic achieves up to 1.73X speedup over an iGPU baseline, 1.78X over an NPU baseline, and 2.05X over frameworks such as \texttt{llama.cpp}, while reducing energy by up to 45.3%. It also improves performance over prior heterogeneous edge AI solutions by up to 2.35X.

</details>


### [90] [Proceedings of HLPP 2026: 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12917)
*Chong Li,Corinne Ancourt,Gaétan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是HLPP 2026国际研讨会（第19届高级并行编程与应用研讨会）的论文集前言，介绍了会议的背景、主题范围、2026年新增的AI相关方向以及论文评审和编辑信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为HLPP 2026研讨会论文集提供前言说明，阐述会议主题和论文收录情况。

Method: 举行了为期两天的国际研讨会，通过单盲评审流程（每篇论文3位程序委员会成员评审）筛选出10篇论文，并按会议议程顺序编排。

Result: 收录了10篇经过同行评审的论文，涵盖并行编程模型、语言、库、算法骨架、编译器、运行时系统等传统主题，以及2026年新增的AI系统并行编程与性能、AI辅助并行代码生成等新方向。

Conclusion: HLPP 2026于2026年7月9-10日在法国巴黎亨利·庞加莱研究所召开，由Chong Li、Corinne Ancourt和Gaetan Hains编辑完成论文集。

Abstract: This volume contains the ten peer-reviewed papers presented at HLPP 2026, the 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications, held on 9-10 July 2026 at the Institut Henri Poincare in Paris, France. The symposium covers high-level approaches to parallel programming: programming models, languages, libraries, algorithmic skeletons, compilers, and runtime systems for multi-core, GPU, and distributed platforms. The 2026 edition extended this scope towards artificial intelligence, with new topics on the parallel programming and performance of AI systems and on AI-assisted generation of parallel code, and opened with a dedicated session on parallelism and AI. Papers were selected through a single-blind review process, with three Program Committee reviews per submission, and appear in the order of the symposium program. Edited by Chong Li, Corinne Ancourt, and Gaetan Hains.

</details>

### [91] [Optimal Adaptive Market Making: A Theoretical Framework for High-Yield Liquidity Provision in Perpetual Futures Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11888)
*Minmin Zeng,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了零做市商费用的永续期货市场中做市商问题的严格理论框架，通过随机最优控制模型推导最优买卖价差与库存对冲策略，统一并扩展了经典做市模型以适应现代去中心化交易所微观结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化永续期货交易所的零费用做市商机制缺乏严格的理论分析，传统做市模型（如Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia等）无法直接适用于新型去中心化交易场所的微观结构特征。

Method: 采用随机最优控制理论，在滤波概率空间上建模自适应买卖价差和跨交易所库存对冲决策；推导PnL分解定理、HJB方程及验证定理；通过五个无量纲参数刻画High-APY盈利区域，推导Master APY公式；分析零费用经济学、最优进出阈值、跨交易所对冲策略三元组、稳健性边界、指数型回撤概率界、遍历库存分布与贝叶斯自适应估计、Kelly最优杠杆与破产边界、多资产组合配置与分散化饱和结果。

Result: 推导出PnL分解定理、HJB方程及验证定理、Master APY公式、对冲策略三元组、稳健性边界、APY-VaR恒等式、遍历库存分布、Kelly最优杠杆与多资产组合分散化饱和结果，并通过23张数值分析图表展示盈利与非盈利区域之间的相变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一理论框架，能够刻画去中心化永续期货市场中的最优做市策略，并揭示了盈利与非盈利区域的相变特征，为实践提供理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [92] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了非平稳环境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL），重点探讨在部署策略参数固定不变的情况下，如何通过上下文信息适应环境变化——即策略需同时推断当前决策规则及历史证据中哪些部分仍然有效。论文围绕“什么在变化、变化如何展开、变化对智能体有多可观测”三个问题组织文献，并对非平稳ICRL与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习等领域的关系进行了梳理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL综述主要围绕预训练目标、架构、上下文格式、评估协议和理论机制进行组织，而对非平稳（环境变化）场景的关注相对不足。在变化环境中，累积的上下文不仅仅是关于固定任务的更多证据——奖励规范、转移核、观测通道、行动接口、约束模型或演示与记忆分布都可能与当前阶段失配，导致先前有用的上下文变得过时、误导，或在旧阶段回归时再次有用。因此需要专门对非平稳ICRL进行系统综述。

Method: 本文将非平稳ICRL定义为：在部署策略参数固定不变的情况下，通过上下文实现适应——策略必须同时推断当前决策规则以及累积证据中哪些部分仍然支持该规则。论文将非平稳ICRL与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习、值/模型感知的ICRL以及奖励反馈智能体相关联，并沿着三个核心问题组织文献：(1) 什么在变化？(2) 变化如何展开？(3) 变化对智能体有多可观测？

Result: 该综述系统性地梳理了非平稳ICRL领域的研究现状，建立了统一的分类框架，明确了非平稳ICRL与相关领域的关系，并识别了已有研究的主要覆盖范围与缺口。

Conclusion: 非平稳ICRL是一个重要但尚未被充分探索的方向，其核心挑战在于如何在参数固定的情况下，仅通过上下文信息应对环境动态变化。本文提供的分类框架（变化的内容、方式、可观测性）为未来研究提供了组织性视角。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window.
